TL;DR: 该理论研究多量子比特系统中单粒子扇区初始态的存活概率，发现衰变特性对哈密顿算符扩展态的存在敏感，但与相互作用是否为混沌高斯正交系综无关，揭示了纯幺正动力学中平衡化的一般机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于基于超导电路的多量子比特平台的实验室发展，需要理论工具来分析这些系统中量子态的动力学行为，特别是存活概率这一基本物理量。

Method: 使用随机矩阵理论的基本概念，在数学模型中推导存活概率的解析表达式。这些模型虽然简化，但已被证明能为实验数据提供相关基准。研究比较了高斯正交系综（常解释为混沌动力学模型）和解析可解链两种相互作用描述。

Result: 发现存活概率的衰变特性对哈密顿算符是否具有扩展态敏感，但不依赖于相互作用是高斯正交系综还是解析可解链。这表明了纯幺正动力学中平衡化出现的一般机制。在初始态投影到哈密顿算符大部分扩展本征态的假设下，将经典的Kac-Mazur-Montroll返回时间估计扩展到量子存活概率。

Conclusion: 存活概率的衰变行为主要取决于哈密顿算符本征态的扩展性质，而非相互作用的混沌特性。这为理解多量子比特系统中量子动力学的平衡化机制提供了理论见解，对超导量子比特平台的实验研究具有指导意义。

Abstract: We present a theoretical study of the survival probability of a state initially prepared in the one-particle sector of a multi-qubit system. The motivation for our work is the ongoing laboratory development of multi-qubit platforms based on superconducting circuits. Using elementary concepts of random matrix theory, we obtain analytic expressions for the survival probability in mathematical models of platforms which, albeit stylized, have been previously shown to provide relevant benchmarks for experimental data. In particular, we show that the decay properties are sensitive to the property of the Hamilton operator to have extended states. The survival probability does not appear instead to depend on whether the interaction between qubits is described by a Gaussian orthogonal ensemble (often interpreted as a model of ''chaotic'' dynamics) or is modeled by an analytically solvable chain. We interpret this phenomenon as a manifestation of a general mechanism for the emergence of equilibration in purely unitary dynamics. Finally, under the same hypothesis of an initial preparation with projection on a large fraction of the extended eigenstates of the Hamilton operator, we show how to extend the classical Kac-Mazur-Montroll estimate of the return time to the quantum survival probability.

</details>


### [87] [Nonlinearity-Induced Thouless Pumping in Quasiperiodic Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00639)
*Xiao-Xiao Hu,Dun Zhao,Hong-Gang Luo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 非线性拓扑泵浦在准周期晶格中的实现，通过局域非线性自洽势重构晶格势，诱导出拓扑结构，实现准量子化的Thouless泵浦


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非线性拓扑泵浦在准周期晶格中的对应现象，研究非线性如何诱导复杂晶格势中的拓扑行为

Method: 在准周期晶格中研究带隙孤子的非线性拓扑泵浦，通过局域非线性自洽势导致晶格势重构，产生诱导拓扑结构控制孤子动力学

Result: 实现了准量子化的Thouless泵浦，孤子可绝热占据单个拓扑能带；发现本征晶格扰动会破坏能带占据，使孤子进入非量子化漂移区；即使在漂移区，孤子输运也受临界有理近似的拓扑性质约束

Conclusion: 通过调节非线性或晶格缩放，可在拓扑泵浦、漂移和局域化之间实现可控切换，揭示了非线性诱导复杂晶格势中拓扑行为的机制

Abstract: Nonlinear Thouless pumping has been established in periodic lattices; its counterpart in quasiperiodic lattices remains unexplored. Here, we show a nonlinear topological pumping of gap solitons in quasiperiodic lattices where the local nonlinear self-consistent potentials lead to a lattice potential reconstruction; as a result, an emergent topological structure induced by this local reconstruction governs the dynamics of the gap solitons. This enables solitons to adiabatically occupy a single topological band, realizing quasi-quantized Thouless pumping. In addition, the intrinsic lattice perturbations disrupt this band occupation, which drives solitons into a non-quantized drifting regime. However, even in this regime, we also find that the soliton transport is constrained by the topological properties of a critical rational approximant. Tuning nonlinearity or lattice scaling reveals a controllable switching among topological pumping, drifting, and localization. Our work uncovers a mechanism for nonlinearity-induced topological behavior in complex lattice potentials.

</details>


### [88] [Quantum Algorithms for Gibbs Expectation of Non-log-concave and Heavy-tailed Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00656)
*Xinmiao Li,Jin-Peng Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出无偏量子采样与估计框架，用于经典吉布斯期望计算，推广分区函数估计，处理非对数凹分布（特别是重尾分布），在耗散性等弱假设下实现量子加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子算法在分区函数估计中存在偏差，且通常需要强凸性等严格假设。需要建立更通用的无偏量子采样框架，处理更广泛的分布类型（包括重尾分布），并在更弱的假设下实现量子加速。

Method: 建立系统化的无偏量子采样与估计框架，推广量子加速多级蒙特卡洛（QA-MLMC）方法，通过测度变换和Girsanov定理（利用Radon-Nikodym导数）消除离散化和时间截断带来的所有偏差。

Result: 实现量子复杂度$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-1})$（误差$ε$），而经典MLMC需要$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$，且现有量子算法在更强假设下仍产生有偏估计。该框架还能处理某些重尾分布的无偏量子采样与估计。

Conclusion: 提出的统一框架为统计、机器学习和金融等领域的实际应用提供了无偏量子采样与估计方法，推动了随机过程量子加速向更实际场景的发展。

Abstract: We establish a systematic framework of unbiased quantum sampling and estimation protocols for the classical Gibbs expectation. This framework generalizes existing approaches to the partition function estimation and has broader applications in various fields. We consider sampling and estimation for a wide class of non-log-concave distributions, particularly heavy-tailed ones, under relaxed assumptions beyond strong convexity, such as dissipativity.
  We develop an unbiased extension of quantum-accelerated multilevel Monte Carlo (QA-MLMC) to eliminate all biases from discretization and time truncation, together with introducing a change-of-measure approach and the Girsanov theorem via Radon-Nikodym derivatives. As a result, our approach achieves quantum complexity $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-1})$ within error $ε$, whereas the classical MLMC requires $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ and existing quantum algorithms yield biased estimators under stronger assumptions. Furthermore, our unified framework enables unbiased quantum sampling and estimation for certain heavy-tailed distributions after transformation. We provide several concrete applications of our approach in statistics, machine learning, and finance, towards more practical scenarios of the quantum acceleration of stochastic processes.

</details>


### [89] [Learning Hidden Structures in Open Quantum Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00711)
*Alexander Teretenkov,Sergey Kuznetsov,Alexander Pechen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种机器学习方法，用于在受限实验访问条件下识别开放量子动力学的隐藏结构特征，专注于推断有效马尔可夫演化背后的不变代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法大多关注动力学行为的检测或预测，但缺乏对底层代数结构的推断能力。需要一种能够在测量受限、对称性和超选择规则约束下识别不变代数结构的方法。

Method: 采用*-代数描述可观测量的测量限制、对称性和超选择规则。将学习问题表述为从多时间测量序列的最大似然估计，将不变子代数的代数类型（特别是退相干自由子代数）作为离散结构假设处理。

Result: 在多个合成模型和波导量子电动力学系统中验证了方法的可行性，能够直接从测量数据中识别非平凡的中介代数结构。

Conclusion: 该方法为在实验受限条件下推断开放量子动力学的隐藏代数结构提供了有效框架，特别适用于识别退相干自由子代数等不变结构。

Abstract: We introduce a machine-learning approach for identifying hidden structural features of open quantum dynamics under restricted experimental access. Unlike most existing data-driven methods which focus on detection or prediction of dynamical behavior, our framework targets the inference of invariant algebraic structures underlying the effective Markovian evolution. Measurement limitations, symmetries, and superselection rules are incorporated through a $*$-algebraic description of accessible observables. The learning problem is formulated as maximum-likelihood estimation from multi-time measurement sequences, where the algebraic type of an invariant subalgebra - articularly a decoherence-free subalgebra - is treated as a discrete structural hypothesis. The feasibility of the approach is illustrated on multiple synthetic models and a waveguide quantum electrodynamics system, where nontrivial intermediate algebraic structures are identified directly from measurement data.

</details>


### [90] [Engineering a Phase-Noise-Based Quantum Random Number Generator for Real-Time Secure Applications: Design, Validation, and Scalability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00741)
*Anurag K. S. V.,Shubham Chouhan,K. Srinivasan,G. Raghavan,Kanaka Raju P*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文开发了一种基于相位噪声的高速高效量子随机数生成系统，利用半导体激光器的自发发射量子噪声，实现了2.0 Gbps的原始数据生成速率和1.0 Gbps的后处理生成速率，通过了NIST和Diehard测试，技术成熟度达到TRL 7级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机数生成器在密码学和仿真等应用中至关重要。传统伪随机数生成器存在可预测性问题，而真随机数生成器通常速度较慢。量子随机数生成器利用量子物理的固有随机性，能够提供高速、高质量的真正随机数，满足实时安全应用的需求。

Method: 采用基于相位噪声的量子随机数生成方法，利用工作在阈值附近的半导体激光器（线宽约5.23 GHz）的自发发射量子噪声。使用自外差测量技术配合约48 cm的光纤延迟线，实现2.0 Gbps的原始数据生成。为确保输出均匀随机性，采用内部开发的Toeplitz强提取器等稳健提取技术进行后处理。

Result: 系统实现了2.0 Gbps的原始数据生成速率和1.0 Gbps的后处理生成速率。随机性验证显示，所有NIST和Diehard统计测试均在标准置信水平下通过。系统技术成熟度达到TRL 7级，接近TRL 8级，适合实时安全应用。

Conclusion: 成功开发了一种高速、高效的基于相位噪声的量子随机数生成系统，能够满足密码学密钥生成和随机建模等实时安全应用的需求。系统的高技术成熟度表明其已接近商业化部署阶段。

Abstract: Random Number Generators (RNGs) are crucial for applications ranging from cryptography to simulations. Depending on the source of randomness, RNGs are classified into Pseudo-Random Number Generators (PRNGs), True Random Number Generators (TRNGs), and Quantum Random Number Generators (QRNGs). This work presents the end-to-end development of a high-speed, high-efficiency, phase-noise-based QRNG system that taps into the quantum phase noise of a single-frequency laser, with randomness originating from spontaneous emission. Using a self-heterodyne measurement with a semiconductor laser (linewidth $\approx$ 5.23 $GHz$) operated near threshold and a $\sim$48 $cm$ fiber delay line, a raw data generation rate of 2.0 $Gbps$ is achieved. To ensure uniform randomness in the QRNG output, robust extraction techniques developed in-house, such as the Toeplitz Strong Extractor (TSE), are used. Randomness validation using the NIST and Diehard test suites confirms that all statistical tests pass at standard confidence levels. The developed system achieves a post-processed generation rate of 1.0 $Gbps$ in operation and attains a Technology Readiness Level (TRL) of 7, approaching TRL 8, making it suitable for real-time secure applications such as cryptographic key generation and stochastic modeling.

</details>


### [91] [Lower Bounds on Coherent State Rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00766)
*Florian Cottier,Ulysse Chabaud*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了近似相干态秩的下界，提出了基于低秩逼近理论的技术，获得了单模和多模量子态的下界，并证明了n模Fock态的超多项式下界，为玻色子采样的经典模拟建立了无条件障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似相干态秩是模拟连续变量玻色子量子计算经典复杂度的关键度量，但对其下界了解甚少，即使是基本量子态族。本文旨在系统研究近似相干态秩的下界，特别是为玻色子采样的经典模拟建立理论障碍。

Method: 1. 引入基于低秩逼近理论的技术，获得任意单模态近似相干态秩的通用下界；2. 将该技术应用于单模态，完全表征有限近似相干态秩的所有单模态；3. 将单模下界提升到多模情况，适用于多模Fock态的有限叠加；4. 利用与永久式的联系，通过证明近似多线性公式的代数复杂度超多项式，获得n模Fock态的超多项式下界。

Result: 1. 获得了单模态近似相干态秩的通用下界技术；2. 完全表征了有限近似相干态秩的所有单模态，获得了压缩态和Fock态有限叠加的解析表达式；3. 将单模下界推广到多模Fock态有限叠加；4. 证明了n模Fock态|1⟩^⊗n的近似相干态秩具有超多项式下界，为玻色子采样的相干态分解经典模拟建立了无条件障碍。

Conclusion: 本文系统建立了近似相干态秩的下界理论，填补了该领域的重要空白。通过连接低秩逼近理论和代数复杂度，不仅获得了具体量子态的下界结果，更重要的是为玻色子采样的经典模拟建立了理论障碍，将玻色子系统的非经典性与代数复杂度的核心问题联系起来。

Abstract: The approximate coherent state rank is the minimal number of (classical) coherent states required to approximate a continuous-variable bosonic quantum state and directly relates to the classical complexity of simulating bosonic computations. Despite its importance, little is known about lower bounds on this quantity, even for basic families of states. In this work, we initiate a systematic study of lower bounds on the approximate coherent state rank. Our contributions are as follows.
  (i) We introduce a technique based on low-rank approximation theory yielding generic lower bounds on the approximate coherent state rank of arbitrary single-mode states.
  (ii) Using this technique, we find a complete characterization of all single-mode states of finite approximate coherent state rank, and we obtain in particular analytical expressions for the approximate coherent state rank of squeezed states and of finite superpositions of Fock states.
  (iii) We further show that our single-mode lower bounds can be lifted to multimode lower bounds for finite superpositions of multimode Fock states.
  (iv) Finally, we prove a super-polynomial lower bound on the approximate coherent state rank of the $n$-mode Fock state $|1\rangle^{\otimes n}$, by exploiting a connection to the permanent. To do so, we show that the algebraic complexity of approximate multi-linear formulas for the permanent is super-polynomial, building upon the proof of a lower bound for exact formulas due to [Raz, JACM 2009].
  Our results establish an unconditional barrier to efficient classical simulation of Boson Sampling via coherent state decompositions and connect non-classicality of bosonic quantum systems to central questions in algebraic complexity.

</details>


### [92] [Vibrationally Induced Resonances in Lasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00798)
*Kai Müller,Kimmo Luoma,Christian Schäfer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了分子尺度纳米激光器中振动结构对激光行为的影响，揭示了传统"非相干驱动"近似方法的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着光学电路和光源（如激光器）的持续微型化，纳米激光器可能仅由几个分子组成。这种少发射体激光器具有低能耗、快速响应和微小尺寸的优势，可集成到任何设备或生物组织中。然而，在分子尺度上，振动结构对激光行为的影响尚未得到充分理解。

Method: 采用最近开发的堆叠层次方法，该方法基于第一性原理，通过显式考虑整个振动流形来研究少分子在等离子体腔中的激光行为。分析了斯托克斯位移、驱动强度和发射体数量对激光强度共振的影响。

Result: 研究发现激光强度存在依赖于斯托克斯位移、驱动强度和发射体数量的共振现象。这些结果揭示了传统"非相干驱动"近似方法的局限性，该近似在分子尺度纳米激光器中不再适用。

Conclusion: 该工作确定了普遍存在的"非相干驱动"近似的局限性，并为理解分子尺度纳米激光器奠定了基础。通过考虑完整的振动结构，可以更准确地预测和设计少分子激光器的性能。

Abstract: Optical circuits and light sources, such as lasers, undergo continuous miniaturization. In its extreme, nanolasers might be comprised of only a few molecules confined in plasmonic nanoresonators. Few-emitter lasers promise low energy requirements and fast responses in a footprint that can be inserted into any device or biological tissue. Utilizing the recently developed stacked hierarchy approach, informed from first principles, we demonstrate the impact of vibrational structure on lasing, using the example of few-molecule lasing in plasmonic cavities. Explicitly accounting for the entire vibrational manifold unveils resonances in the laser intensity that depend on the Stokes shift, drive strength, and the number of emitters. Our work identifies the limits of the omnipresent "incoherent drive"-approximation and paves the way for the understanding of nanolasers at the molecular scale.

</details>


### [93] [Unitary Encoding of Thermal States via Thermofield Dynamics on Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00802)
*G. X. A. Petronilo,M. R. Araújo,A. B. M. Souza,Clebson Cruz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种适用于NISQ设备的门基量子算法，用于制备热场动力学有限温度真空态并追踪其实时演化，电路深度随系统尺寸线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期量子设备受热效应限制严重，导致退相干和有限温度下的相干控制受限。需要开发适用于近期限子设备的量子算法来模拟热系统。

Method: 开发了基于门的量子算法，通过单量子比特旋转和最近邻CNOT门制备热场动力学的有限温度真空态，电路深度与系统尺寸呈线性关系，适合NISQ设备。

Result: 在PennyLane模拟器上验证：自旋-1/2粒子在磁场中的磁化强度与精确解M(β)=tanh(βω/2)达到机器精度一致；相干进动获得温度依赖性阻尼，与解析TFD预测定量匹配。

Conclusion: 该工作为热量子模拟提供了可直接部署的工具箱，为在近期限子设备上研究耗散相变、量子热力学和热机器学习模型开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum computing has attracted the attention of the scientific community in the past few decades. However, despite some relevant advantages, near-term quantum devices remain severely limited by thermal effects, which induce decoherence and restrict coherent control at finite temperature. In this regard, this work reports a gate-based quantum algorithm that prepares the finite-temperature vacuum of Thermofield Dynamics (TFD) and tracks its real-time evolution. The circuit depth scales linearly with system size and requires only single-qubit rotations and nearest-neighbor CNOT gates, making it NISQ-friendly. We benchmark the protocol on the PennyLane simulator: magnetization of a spin-$1/2$ particle in a magnetic field agrees with the exact result $M(β)=\tanh(βω/2)$ to machine precision, and the coherent precession acquires a temperature-dependent damping that quantitatively matches the analytical TFD prediction. Our work provides a ready-to-deploy toolbox for thermal quantum simulations and opens a route to study dissipative phase transitions, quantum thermodynamics and thermal machine-learning models on near-term devices.

</details>


### [94] [Highly-Parallel Atom-Detection Accelerator for Tweezer-Based Neutral Atom Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00816)
*Jonas Winklmann,Yian Yu,Xiaorang Guo,Korbinian Staudacher,Martin Schulz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于镊子基中性原子量子计算机的高度并行原子检测加速器，通过算法级优化和FPGA实现，将256×256像素荧光图像分析时间缩短至115μs，相比原始CPU实现获得34.9倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子量子计算机中，控制和测量单个原子及其状态通常需要多次成像和图像分析过程，这是计算过程中最耗时的任务，显著增加了整体循环时间。现有方法在图像分析效率上存在瓶颈，限制了量子计算的整体性能。

Method: 基于现有状态重构方法，结合算法级优化和现场可编程门阵列（FPGA）实现，最大化并行性并减少图像分析过程的运行时间。具体包括：1）引入预取机制以提高可扩展性；2）定制总线传输以支持大带宽；3）在Xilinx UltraScale+ FPGA上实现高度并行化设计。

Result: 在Xilinx UltraScale+ FPGA上测试，设计能够分析256×256像素荧光图像仅需115μs，相比原始CPU基线实现34.9倍加速，相比优化后的CPU基线实现6.3倍加速。此外，加速器能够在不同原子阵列尺寸下保持一致的资源利用率。

Conclusion: 提出的FPGA加速器显著减少了中性原子量子计算机的图像分析时间，支持可扩展的原子阵列尺寸，为构建可扩展且完全集成的FPGA控制系统做出了贡献，有助于推进中性原子量子计算平台的发展。

Abstract: Neutral atom quantum computers (NAQCs) are among the most promising computational platforms for quantum computing. Controlling and measuring individual atoms and their states, which often requires multiple imaging and image-analysis procedures, is typically the most time-consuming task during computation and contributes significantly to overall cycle times. To resolve this challenge, we propose a highly-parallel atom-detection accelerator for tweezer-based NAQCs. Our design builds on an existing state-reconstruction method and combines an algorithm-level optimization with a Field Programmable Gate Array (FPGA) implementation to maximize parallelism and reduce the run time of the image-analysis process. We identify and overcome several challenges for an FPGA implementation, such as introducing a prefetching mechanism to improve scalability and customizing bus transfers to support large bandwidths. Tested on a Xilinx UltraScale+ FPGA, our design can analyze a 256x256-pixel fluorescence image in just 115mus, achieving 34.9x and 6.3x speedups over the original and optimized CPU baseline, respectively. Moreover, our accelerator can maintain consistent resource utilization across various atom array sizes, contributing to the ongoing efforts toward scalable and fully integrated FPGA-based control systems for NAQCs.

</details>


### [95] [Quantum Simulation of Cranked Zirconium Isotopes: A Fixed-N Approach with a Structured Number-Conserving Ansatz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00844)
*Abhishek,Nabeel Salim,P. Arumugam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究采用量子计算方法模拟了Nilsson+配对哈密顿量的转动，使用VQE和结构化粒子数守恒的ansatz，提出了新的配对相干性诊断Δ_coh，并应用于Zr同位素分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是开发一种量子计算方法来模拟核物理中的转动现象，特别是在固定变形网格上的Nilsson+配对哈密顿量。传统方法在处理配对相干性时存在局限性，需要新的诊断工具来分析固定粒子数下的配对行为。

Method: 方法包括：1) 将多体Routhian通过Jordan-Wigner变换映射到量子比特；2) 使用变分量子本征求解器(VQE)在截断活性空间(M)中进行最小化；3) 采用结构化粒子数守恒的单双激发ansatz，其中双激发实现配对转移，单激发限制在活性Nilsson基的非零科里奥利耦合图中；4) 引入固定粒子数下的配对相干性诊断Δ_coh = G√∑_{k≠l}|⟨P_k^†P_l⟩|。

Result: 对于Zr同位素的研究显示：¹⁸⁰Zr在δ*≈-0.25处表现出稳定的扁椭球最小值；¹⁸²Zr显示出最强的转动演化；¹⁸⁴Zr保持稳健的长椭球最小值，具有最大的中子配对相干性。M=6和M=8的比较显示稳定趋势但存在可见偏移，未声称活性空间收敛。

Conclusion: 结构化固定粒子数ansatz能够捕捉一致的同位素趋势，并通过Δ_coh为分析配对提供了实用框架。这些结果反映了当前截断模型而非收敛的光谱学，为量子计算在核物理模拟中的应用提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: We present a methodological study of quantum simulation of cranking in a Nilsson $+$ pairing Hamiltonian on a fixed deformation grid. The many-body Routhian is mapped to qubits via the Jordan--Wigner transformation and minimized using the Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) in a truncated active space $(M)$. We employ a structured, number-conserving singles-and-doubles ansatz: double excitations implement pair transfer, while singles are restricted to the nonzero Coriolis-coupling graph of the active Nilsson basis. For $M=8$, this yields 42 parameters while preserving particle number exactly.
  Exact number conservation enforces $\langle P_k \rangle = 0$, so the conventional pairing gap $Δ_κ\propto G\left|\sum_k \langle P_k \rangle\right|$ vanishes identically. We instead introduce a fixed-$N$ pairing-coherence diagnostic, \[ Δ_{\mathrm{coh}} = G \sqrt{\sum_{k \neq l} \left| \langle P_k^\dagger P_l \rangle \right|}, \] used as a scalar measure of off-diagonal pair coherence rather than a BCS gap.
  We study even-even $^{80,82,84}$Zr. $^{80}$Zr shows a stable oblate minimum at $δ^\ast \approx -0.25$; $^{82}$Zr exhibits the strongest rotational evolution; $^{84}$Zr retains a robust prolate minimum with the largest neutron pairing coherence. These results reflect the present truncated model rather than converged spectroscopy. A cranked BCS calculation on the same grid serves as a qualitative baseline. Comparisons between $M=6$ and $M=8$ show stable trends but visible shifts, so no active-space convergence is claimed. The structured fixed-$N$ ansatz thus captures consistent isotope trends and provides a practical framework to analyze pairing via $Δ_{\mathrm{coh}}$.

</details>


### [96] [Conditions for 3-partite and 4-partite genuine entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00875)
*Mark Hillery*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于二分纠缠检测的额外充分条件来判断三或四粒子系统的真正纠缠


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多粒子系统（三或四粒子）的纠缠结构复杂，可能存在仅子集内部纠缠或叠加态的情况。判断是否为真正纠缠（所有粒子全局纠缠）具有挑战性，现有检测条件有限，需要补充更有效的检测方法。

Method: 基于二分纠缠检测方法，推导出适用于三或四粒子系统的额外充分条件，用于判断真正纠缠状态。

Result: 提出了新的充分条件，能够检测GHZ态等真正纠缠状态，补充了现有纠缠检测方法的不足。

Conclusion: 基于二分纠缠检测的充分条件为判断多粒子系统的真正纠缠提供了有效工具，有助于更准确地识别全局纠缠状态。

Abstract: A system of three or four particle can be entangled in a number of different ways. It may be the case that only subsets of the particles are entangled, and these subsets are not entangled with each other. It may also be the case that the state is the sum of states in which entanglement only exists within subsets. If this is not the case, the state is said to be genuinely entangled. GHZ states, for example, are genuinely entangled. Deciding whether a state is genuinely entangled is not simple, but conditions do exist to detect it. Here we would like to propose additional sufficient conditions based on one for bipartite entanglement.

</details>


### [97] [Two Problems on Quantum Computing in Finite Abelian Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00929)
*Ulises Pastor-Díaz,José M. Tornero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出并解决了有限阿贝尔群中的两个问题：隐藏子群问题和完全平衡像问题，使用量子计算技术，特别是布尔对象转换和广义相位回踢技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限阿贝尔群中的计算问题，特别是隐藏子群问题（Simon的经典工作）和新引入的完全平衡像问题，这些问题与群态射等映射类相关，旨在探索量子计算在群论问题中的应用潜力。

Method: 采用两步法：首先将问题转换为更适合量子计算的布尔对象，然后设计定制算法利用广义相位回踢技术来解决问题。

Result: 成功解决了有限阿贝尔群中的隐藏子群问题和完全平衡像问题，证明了所提量子计算方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文展示了量子计算技术在解决有限阿贝尔群相关问题中的强大能力，特别是通过布尔转换和广义相位回踢技术的组合应用，为群论问题的量子算法设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: In the context of finite Abelian groups two problems are presented and solved using quantum computing techniques. The first is the well--known Hidden Subgroup Problem, originally solved by Simon in a landmark work. The second is the Fully Balanced Image Problem, originally introduced by the authors (joint with J. Ossorio--Castillo), which is related to a certain class of mappings (which contains strictly, for instance, the family of group morphisms).
  Both problems are tackled using a combination of two techniques: first, a conversion into Boolean objects, better suited for quantum computing arguments, and subsequently a custom--tailored algorithm which takes advantage of the Generalised Phase--Kick Back technique.

</details>


### [98] [Two-Qubit Implementation of QAOA for MAX-CUT on an NV-Center Quantum Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00949)
*Leon E. Röscher,Talía L. M. Lezama,Luca Cimino,Jonah vom Hofe,Riccardo Bassoli,Frank H. P. Fitzek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在室温NV中心量子处理器上实现了最小非平凡MAX-CUT问题的量子近似优化算法(QAOA)原理验证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在室温NV中心平台上验证QAOA算法的核心要素，为未来相位跟踪、相干保持控制和更大规模问题扩展建立基准

Method: 使用单个NV⁻中心的电子自旋和¹⁴N核自旋编码双量子比特寄存器，采用MAX-CUT的最小化表述，实现单层QAOA ansatz，利用原生纠缠和单量子比特控制操作，通过平均荧光信号重建计算基态布居

Result: 成功在室温NV中心平台上实现了QAOA核心要素，通过扫描变分参数获得了实验QAOA代价景观，证明了该平台实现QAOA的可行性

Conclusion: 该工作展示了NV中心平台实现QAOA的潜力，为未来改进相位跟踪、相干保持控制和扩展到更大问题规模奠定了基础

Abstract: We report a proof-of-principle implementation of the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) for the smallest nontrivial MAX-CUT instance on an NV-center-based quantum processor operating at room temperature. The two-qubit register is encoded in the electron spin and the ${}^{14}\mathrm{N}$ nuclear spin of a single NV$^-$ center. Using a minimization formulation of MAX-CUT, we implement a single-layer QAOA ansatz with native entangling and single-qubit control operations. Because the optical readout of the NV$^-$ center is not projective in the computational basis, we reconstruct computational-basis populations from averaged fluorescence signals and use them to determine the experimental QAOA cost landscape by scanning the variational parameters. These results show that the core elements of QAOA can be realized on this platform and establish a baseline for future improvements in phase tracking, coherence-preserving control, and scaling to larger problem sizes.

</details>


### [99] [Properties of multiqubit variational quantum states representing weighted graphs and their computing with quantum programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00958)
*Kh. P. Gnatenko,A. Kaczmarek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多量子比特变分量子态，这些态可视为加权量子图态，通过单层变分电路构建，包含RX旋转和RZZ纠缠门，对应任意结构的图。作者推导了任意结构量子图态的几何纠缠度量并计算了量子关联子，发现这些量与图中对应顶点的度数直接相关。以星图K_{1,4}为例，在AerSimulator上进行含噪声量子计算验证，结果与理论预测吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究图结构与量子性质之间的关联，探索通过量子计算研究经典图论问题的可能性。具体动机包括：1) 建立量子图态与经典图结构之间的直接联系；2) 开发基于变分量子电路的量子图态构造方法；3) 验证量子计算在分析图论性质方面的应用潜力。

Method: 采用单层变分量子电路构造加权量子图态，电路包含RX旋转门和RZZ纠缠门，对应任意结构的图。对于任意结构的量子图态，推导了几何纠缠度量并计算了量子关联子。以星图K_{1,4}为例，在AerSimulator上进行含噪声量子计算实验验证理论结果。

Result: 研究发现几何纠缠度量和量子关联子与图中对应顶点的度数直接相关。对于星图K_{1,4}的量子态，在AerSimulator上的含噪声量子计算结果与理论预测吻合良好。这证实了图结构与量子性质之间的直接联系。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了量子图态与经典图结构之间的明确联系，证明了量子计算可用于研究图论性质。变分量子电路构造的加权量子图态为通过量子系统分析经典图结构提供了有效框架，为量子图论研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We study multiqubit variational quantum states that can be considered as weighted quantum graph states. These states are constructed as single-layer variational circuits with $RX$ rotations and $RZZ$ entangling gates, corresponding to graphs of arbitrary structure. In general case of quantum graph states of arbitrary structure we derive the geometric measure of entanglement and evaluate quantum correlators. It is shown that these quantities are directly related to the degrees of the corresponding vertices in graph. As an example, we analyze the state associated with the star graph $K_{1,4}$ using noisy quantum computing on the AerSimulator. The results are in good agreement with theoretical predictions. These findings demonstrate a connection between graph structure and quantum properties, enabling the study of classical graphs via quantum computing.

</details>


### [100] [A Factorization Identity for Twisted Multinomial Coefficients with Application to Pilot States in Hamiltonian Decoded Quantum Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01022)
*Pawel Wocjan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文引入扭曲多项式系数，在满足前驱均匀性条件下证明其可分解为高斯二项式的乘积，并将结果应用于量子算法中的导频态制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准q-多项式系数仅有一个变形参数q，限制了其应用范围。研究需要推广到更一般的权重结构，特别是为了量子算法HDQI中的导频态制备问题。

Method: 引入扭曲多项式系数，其中每个逆序对(i,j)携带由斜对称矩阵Ω决定的权重ω_ij。在满足前驱均匀性条件(ω_ij = q_j对所有i<j)下，证明扭曲多项式系数可分解为高斯二项式的乘积。

Result: 在Ω满足前驱均匀性条件下，扭曲多项式系数可精确分解为高斯二项式的乘积：∏_j binom(ℓ_j,k_j)_{q_j}。该结果为纯组合恒等式，对任意非零复数q_j成立，无需代数约束。

Conclusion: 扭曲多项式系数在前驱均匀性条件下的分解公式推广了标准q-多项式系数，为HDQI量子算法中的导频态制备提供了精确的矩阵乘积态表示，但完整协议的实现仍需解决相关哈密顿量的高效解码问题。

Abstract: The $q$-multinomial coefficient, a classical object in enumerative combinatorics, counts permutations of multisets weighted by the number of inversions, with a single deformation parameter $q$. We introduce the twisted multinomial coefficient, in which each inversion between letters $i$ and $j$ carries a pair-dependent weight $ω_{ij}$ determined by a skew-symmetric matrix $Ω$. In general, no closed-form evaluation is known. Our main result is that under a natural structural condition on $Ω$ - predecessor-uniformity ($ω_{ij} = q_j$ for all $i<j$) - the twisted multinomial factorizes as a product of Gaussian ($q$-deformed) binomials with site-dependent parameters: $\binom{k}{k_1,\ldots,k_m}_Ω= \prod_j\binom{\ell_j}{k_j}_{q_j}$. This extends the standard product formula for the $q$-multinomial from a single parameter $q$ to $m-1$ independent parameters. The identity is purely combinatorial: it holds for arbitrary $q_j \in \mathbb{C}\setminus\{0\}$ without any algebraic constraints.
  We were led to this identity by studying pilot state preparation in Hamiltonian Decoded Quantum Interferometry (HDQI), a recently proposed quantum algorithm for preparing Gibbs and ground states. As an application, we show that the factorization yields an exact matrix product state (MPS) of bond dimension $k+1$ for the expansion coefficients of $h^k$ in a twisted algebra. We note that this addresses only one component of the HDQI pipeline (pilot state preparation); the full protocol additionally requires efficient decoding of the associated Hamiltonian code, and both components must work in conjunction for Hamiltonians of physical interest. Identifying such Hamiltonians remains an important open problem.

</details>


### [101] [Geometry-induced correlated noise in qLDPC syndrome extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01040)
*Angelo Di Bella*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究几何布线对量子纠错码逻辑性能的影响，发现特定几何布局能抑制单层嵌入惩罚，加权暴露度与逻辑错误率高度相关


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代码和综合征提取调度固定的情况下，探究几何布线是否足以改变相关故障模型并影响逻辑性能，为量子纠错系统的综合优化提供依据

Method: 从几何条件化的同tick相互作用哈密顿量出发，推导双变量自行车布局的受控保留单故障和配对数据故障模型；提出两种几何度量指标；通过电路级蒙特卡洛模拟验证

Result: 双平面有界厚度布局能抑制单层嵌入惩罚，加权暴露度与逻辑错误率高度相关（Spearman相关系数0.893）；在BB72上优化使最坏情况暴露度降低26.11%，在测试的幂律窗口内降低逻辑错误率

Conclusion: 几何布线应与代码、调度和解码器一起优化，几何布局对量子纠错性能有显著影响，需要综合考虑系统设计

Abstract: With code and syndrome-extraction schedule fixed, can routed geometry alone change the correlated fault model enough to impact logical performance? Starting from a geometry-conditioned same-tick interaction Hamiltonian, we derive a controlled retained single-and-pair data-fault model for bivariate-bicycle (BB) layouts. Two geometry metrics emerge in two kernel regimes: under a crossing-local diagnostic kernel, a matching argument reduces the support-level effective fault weight; when every support pair appears in at least one retained round with finite same-round separation, strictly positive kernels saturate the support graph, and weighted exposure becomes the discriminating quantity. Circuit-level Monte Carlo on the $[\![72, 12, 6]\!]$ and $[\![144, 12, 12]\!]$ benchmarks confirms that a biplanar bounded-thickness layout suppresses the monomial single-layer embedding penalty, with weighted exposure tracking logical error rate across 101 operating points (Spearman correlation 0.893). A single-layer logical-family optimization on BB72 reduces worst-case exposure by 26.11% and lowers logical error rate in the tested power-law window. Routed geometry should be optimized together with code, schedule, and decoder.

</details>


### [102] [Distilling Unitary Operations: A No-Go Theorem and Minimal Realization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01048)
*Jiayi Zhao,Yu-Ao Chen,Guocheng Zhen,Chengkai Zhu,Ranyiliu Chen,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究了量子门操作的净化问题，证明了在不定因果序框架下，2槽高阶操作无法普遍净化单量子比特酉操作，而3槽架构是最小可实现非平凡普遍净化的方案，并给出了最优平均保真度和具体量子电路构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理硬件上执行的量子门不可避免地受到环境噪声的影响。虽然状态净化能有效提纯静态量子资源，但量子算法的动态执行需要对操作本身采用高阶方法来缓解错误。本研究探索酉操作净化：利用量子高阶操作来部分恢复被已知噪声模型破坏的未知酉操作的理想作用。

Method: 聚焦于典型的去极化噪声，首先揭示了基本的操作障碍：在不定因果序框架下，证明了没有非平凡的2槽高阶操作能够普遍净化单量子比特酉操作集合。克服这一严格限制后，建立了3槽架构作为非平凡普遍净化的最小实现方案。解析推导了3槽机制下的最优平均保真度，并提供了该最优高阶操作的具体量子电路构造。

Result: 证明了2槽架构无法实现非平凡普遍净化，而3槽架构是最小可实现方案。解析推导的最优平均保真度严格超越了平凡策略，通过系统利用辅助量子比特作为量子存储器来吸收错误。提供了最优高阶操作的具体量子电路实现。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了从噪声门中蒸馏干净操作的严格理论边界，为鲁棒门设计提供了直接的架构洞见。3槽高阶操作架构为量子计算中的错误缓解提供了新的理论框架和实现方案。

Abstract: Quantum gates executed on physical hardware are inevitably degraded by environmental noise. While state purification effectively distills static quantum resources, the dynamic execution of quantum algorithms requires a higher-order approach to mitigate errors on the operations themselves. In this work, we investigate unitary purification: the task of utilizing a quantum higher-order operation to partially restore the ideal action of an unknown unitary corrupted by a known noise model. Focusing on canonical depolarizing noise, we first reveal a fundamental operational obstruction. We prove that within the indefinite causal order framework, no nontrivial 2-slot higher-order operation can universally purify the set of single-qubit unitaries. Overcoming this strict limitation, we establish that a 3-slot architecture provides the minimal realization for non-trivial universal purification. We analytically derive the optimal average fidelity for the 3-slot regime, demonstrating that it strictly surpasses trivial strategies by systematically utilizing ancillary qubits as a quantum memory to absorb errors. Furthermore, we provide a concrete quantum circuit construction for this optimal higher-order operation. Our results establish the strict theoretical boundaries of distilling clean operations from noisy gates, offering immediate architectural insights for robust gate design.

</details>


### [103] [Simulated Bifurcation Quantum Annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01050)
*Jakub Pawłowski,Paweł Tarasiuk,Jan Tuziemski,Łukasz Pawela,Bartłomiej Gardas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: SBQA是一种量子启发的优化算法，通过引入副本间相互作用模拟量子隧穿，在稀疏和崎岖的能量景观上改进模拟分岔算法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟分岔算法(SBM)在稀疏和崎岖的能量景观上表现不佳，需要一种既能保持SBM效率和并行性，又能改进这些困难区域性能的优化算法。

Method: 扩展模拟分岔算法，引入副本间相互作用来模拟量子隧穿效应，推导运动方程，分析参数依赖性，并提出轻量级自动调参策略。

Result: 在大型问题和当前量子硬件相关的小规模实例上的综合基准测试表明，SBQA在SBM表现不佳的稀疏和崎岖区域系统性地改进了性能，同时在各种测试问题族中保持竞争力和通用性。

Conclusion: SBQA是一种实用的量子启发优化启发式算法，为稀疏和崎岖区域提供了更强的经典基线。

Abstract: We introduce Simulated Bifurcation Quantum Annealing (SBQA), a quantum-inspired optimization algorithm that extends simulated bifurcation by incorporating inter-replica interactions to mimic quantum tunneling. SBQA retains the efficiency and parallelism of simulated bifurcation while improving performance on sparse and rugged energy landscapes. We derive its equations of motion, analyze parameter dependence, and propose a lightweight auto-tuning strategy. A comprehensive benchmarking study on both large-scale problems and smaller instances relevant for current quantum hardware shows that SBQA systematically improves on SBM in the sparse and rugged regimes where SBM is known to struggle, while remaining competitive and versatile across a diverse set of tested problem families. These results position SBQA as a practical quantum-inspired optimization heuristic and a stronger classical baseline for the sparse and rugged regimes studied here.

</details>


### [104] [Tsim: Fast Universal Simulator for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01059)
*Rafael Haenel,Xiuzhe Luo,Chen Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Tsim是一个开源的高通量量子电路模拟器，专注于量子纠错，通过ZX图表示电路，利用GPU加速实现高效采样


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要开发一个能够高效模拟含噪声通用量子电路的工具，特别是针对量子纠错应用，同时保持与现有工具（如Stim）的兼容性

Method: 使用ZX图表示量子电路，将泡利通道建模为参数化顶点；通过参数化ZX规则简化图表示，然后编译为GPU加速的向量化采样；支持Stim API和电路格式，并扩展了T门和任意单比特旋转指令

Result: Tsim在一次性编译后，可以在Clifford门数量上线性时间内采样探测器或测量结果，仅在非Clifford门数量上呈指数增长；对于低魔法电路，其吞吐量可与Stim的采样性能相匹配

Conclusion: Tsim是一个功能强大的量子电路模拟器，特别适用于量子纠错研究，通过创新的ZX图表示和GPU加速实现了高效的噪声电路模拟

Abstract: We present Tsim, an open-source high-throughput simulator for universal noisy quantum circuits targeting quantum error correction. Tsim represents quantum circuits as ZX diagrams, where Pauli channels are modeled as parameterized vertices. Diagrams are simplified via parameterized ZX rules, and then compiled for vectorized sampling with GPU acceleration. After the one-time compilation, one can sample detector or measurement shots in linear time in the number of Clifford gates and exponentially only in the number of non-Clifford gates. Tsim implements the Stim API and fully supports the Stim circuit format, extending it with T and arbitrary single-qubit rotation instructions. For low-magic circuits, Tsim throughput can match the sampling performance of Stim.

</details>


### [105] [Understanding Quantum Theory: An Operational Reconstructive Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01095)
*Philip Goyal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文批评当前量子理论解释方法论的局限性，提出基于量子重构程序的替代方法论，并以全同粒子形式体系的重构为例展示该方法的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子理论创立百年后仍缺乏对其描述现实的共识，作者认为这源于主流解释方法论的问题：以量子形式体系为哲学反思起点，忽略了理论的大量内容（建模启发式、实验实践、数学结构），且易受形而上学语言影响。

Method: 提出基于量子重构程序的替代方法论，将量子形式体系的数学内容提炼为物理原理和假设，使其更易于哲学消化，并排除形而上学语言的影响。以全同粒子形式体系的重构为案例研究，展示逐步解释过程。

Result: 该方法论能够确保解释项目考虑所有形式的理论内容。全同粒子重构案例产生了新颖的形而上学图景：全同粒子系统作为整体的潜在部分，这一解释可逐步追溯到基础实验数据和重构的物理假设。

Conclusion: 基于重构的解释方法论优于直接解释量子形式体系的传统方法，能够更全面地捕捉量子理论内容，避免形而上学语言干扰，并为量子实在论提供更坚实的基础。

Abstract: One hundred years after the creation of quantum theory, there is no consensus on the kind of reality that is described by the theory. Here, I attribute the lack of progress to the prevailing interpretative methodology, which invariably takes the quantum formalism as the starting point for philosophical reflection and analysis. I argue that this methodology is particularly inappropriate, for it invariably marginalizes much of the theory's content, both that implicit in modelling heuristics and experimental practices, and that encapsulated in the mathematical structures of its formalism. In addition, the prevailing methodology offers little protection against undue influence by metaphysically-laden language which invariably accompanies the formalism.
  Here, I summarize an alternative methodology whose goal is to ensure that an interpretational project take into account all forms of theoretic content. The methodology harnesses the recent results of the quantum reconstruction program. These results distil the mathematical content of the quantum formalism into physical principles and assumptions, which are more readily philosophically digestible than the formalism itself, and bracket much of its metaphysically-laden language.
  As a case study of reconstruction-based interpretation, I describe the reconstruction of the identical particle formalism, and its step-by-step interpretation, highlighting the key questions that drive the interpretation forwards and the techniques and stances that are employed in each step. The interpretation yields a novel metaphysical profile for systems of identical particles as potential parts of a whole, which can be traced step-by-step to elementary experimental data and the reconstruction's physical postulates and assumptions. I also describe some of the pitfalls that one faces in any attempt to directly interpret the identical particle formalism.

</details>


### [106] [Thermal Entanglement and Out-of-Equilibrium Thermodynamics in 1D Bose gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01157)
*Julia Mathé,Nicky Kai Hong Li,Pharnam Bakhshinezhad,Giuseppe Vitagliano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究一维玻色气体在低能Bogoliubov区域中的平衡与非平衡纠缠，使用协方差矩阵框架检测和量化纠缠，发现热态的最优纠缠见证具有简单的模式分解结构，并研究压缩动力学中纠缠的产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一维量子玻色气体中纠缠的产生和演化，特别是在平衡和非平衡条件下，为理解这类系统中的纠缠行为提供统一的物理图像，并探索纠缠在热力学循环中的作用。

Method: 采用基于协方差矩阵的框架和相关的纠缠单调量，在Gaussian设置下完全通过协方差矩阵表征系统状态。对于热态，确定从协方差矩阵准则产生的最优纠缠见证；对于非平衡动力学，研究由幺正压缩诱导的演化。

Result: 发现热态的最优纠缠见证具有简单的模式分解结构：在正规模式基中是对角的，可以表示为仅两个正规模式不确定性的乘积。在非平衡压缩动力学中，即使从初始可分离的热态也能产生纠缠。在完全绝热演化下，最优见证保持与热态相同的双模结构；在快速压缩下，最优见证变得更加复杂。

Conclusion: 该研究为一维量子玻色气体中纠缠的出现和演化提供了统一且物理直观的图像，并确定了在二次玻色模型分析中具有更广泛相关性的最优见证结构，对理解纠缠在热力学循环中的作用具有重要意义。

Abstract: We investigate entanglement in and out of equilibrium in a one-dimensional Bose gas in its low-energy Bogoliubov regime. In this Gaussian setting, the state is fully characterized by its covariance matrix, which allows us to detect and quantify entanglement using a covariance-based framework and associated entanglement monotones. For thermal states, we determine the optimal entanglement witness arising from the covariance matrix criterion and show that it has a remarkably simple mode-resolved structure: it is diagonal in the normal-mode basis and admits a simple analytic form that can be expressed as a product of only two normal-mode uncertainties. We then study out-of-equilibrium dynamics induced by unitary compression and show that entanglement can be generated even from initially separable thermal states. When the evolution is fully adiabatic, the optimal witness retains the same two-mode structure as in the thermal case. Departing from this regime, i.e., performing increasingly rapid compression, the optimal witness becomes genuinely more intricate. Our methods and results provide a unified and physically intuitive picture of how entanglement emerges and evolves in 1D quantum Bose gases, and identify an optimal witness structure relevant more broadly to the analysis of entanglement in quadratic bosonic models and its role in thermodynamic cycles.

</details>


### [107] [Variational Dynamics of Open Quantum Spin Systems in Phase Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01165)
*Jacopo Tosca,Zejian Li,Francesco Carnazza,Cristiano Ciuti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于自旋相空间表示和变分原理的开放量子自旋系统动力学模拟方法，使用自旋相干态混合表示Husimi-Q函数，允许负系数以捕捉量子关联，无需蒙特卡洛采样即可高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要开发能够高效模拟相互作用开放量子自旋系统动力学的方法，特别是要超越半经典描述的限制，准确捕捉量子关联，并能够扩展到大规模系统。

Method: 基于自旋相空间表示，变分地逼近Husimi-Q函数，使用多维自旋相干态的混合作为变分ansatz，允许混合系数取负值以捕捉量子关联。通过Dirac-Frenkel变分原理推导运动方程，利用ansatz的解析结构实现高效计算，无需蒙特卡洛采样。

Result: 该方法能够准确捕捉横向场量子伊辛模型的全量子动力学和非平衡稳态，与精确对角化结果高度一致。此外，该方法能够高效扩展到大型二维晶格系统，这是其他技术难以处理的区域。

Conclusion: 提出的变分方法为开放量子自旋系统的动力学模拟提供了高效且准确的框架，能够超越半经典描述的限制，捕捉量子关联，并具有良好的可扩展性，适用于大规模系统。

Abstract: We introduce a variational method for simulating the dynamics of interacting open quantum spin systems. The method is based on the spin phase-space representation and variationally targets the Husimi-$Q$ function with an ansatz based on a multi-dimensional mixture of spin-coherent states. Crucially, the mixture coefficients are allowed to take negative values, enabling the faithful capture of quantum correlations beyond semiclassical descriptions. The resulting equations of motion are derived from the Dirac-Frenkel variational principle and can be evaluated efficiently without resorting to Monte Carlo sampling by exploiting the analytical structure of the ansatz. As a first application, we demonstrate that this approach accurately captures both the full quantum dynamics and the non-equilibrium steady states of the transverse-field quantum Ising model, in excellent agreement with exact diagonalization. Furthermore, we show that the method scales efficiently to large two-dimensional lattices, a regime that remains challenging for other techniques.

</details>


### [108] [High Performance Quantum Emulation for Chemistry Applications with Hyperion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01176)
*Olivier Adjoua,Siwar Badreddine,César Feniou,Igor Chollet,Diata Traore,Guillaume Michel,Jean-Philip Piquemal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Hyperion是一个大规模并行、GPU加速的量子模拟器，通过创新的稀疏矩阵-稀疏向量内核和SV-MPS混合策略，实现了32-40量子比特的高精度量子化学模拟，突破了传统模拟器的内存限制和精度瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子硬件的战略需求超过了近期设备的可用性，需要高性能软件模拟器来验证新协议。传统量子化学模拟面临经典内存墙的限制，而纯矩阵乘积态模拟器在压缩精度和计算复杂度之间存在难以调和的矛盾。

Method: 1. 采用定制优化的稀疏矩阵-稀疏向量内核，原生加速精确矩阵向量乘法，支持32量子比特的精确状态向量ADAPT-VQE模拟。2. 提出SV-MPS分区模拟策略：将非相互作用项路由到精确稀疏状态向量核心，将相互作用项委托给MPS引擎，实现36-40量子比特的受控近似模拟。

Result: Hyperion能够在多节点平台上实现32量子比特的严格精确模拟，并通过SV-MPS策略扩展到36-40量子比特，同时显著降低GPU资源需求，在ADAPT-VQE迭代中保持稳健精度，模拟精度接近精确全组态相互作用/完全基组极限。

Conclusion: Hyperion提供了一个高保真度平台，专门用于开发新的量子化学算法，能够以接近精确FCI/CBS极限的精度模拟真实化学系统，为量子算法验证和量子化学研究提供了强大的计算工具。

Abstract: The strategic demand for quantum hardware currently outpaces the availability of near-term devices, necessitating high-performance software emulators to validate novel protocols. We introduce Hyperion, a massively parallel, GPU-accelerated quantum emulator architected to bypass the classical memory walls inherent in strongly correlated quantum chemistry simulations. Hyperion leverages custom-optimized Sparse Matrix-Sparse Vector (SpMspV) kernels to natively accelerate exact matrix-vector multiplications, enabling strictly accurate State-Vector (SV) ADAPT-VQE simulations for up to 32 qubits on multi-node platforms. To scale beyond this hardware limit, we address the trade-off in pure Matrix Product State (MPS) emulators, where standard compression yields severe truncation errors and strict compression triggers intractable tensor rank explosions. We propose a novel partitioned emulation, namely the SV-MPS strategy: by routing non-interacting terms into an exact sparse SV core and delegating interacting terms to the MPS engine, this approach achieves emulation of 36 to 40 qubits with controlled approximations. This partitioning significantly reduces GPU resource requirements while maintaining robust accuracy across ADAPT-VQE iterations. Ultimately, Hyperion offers a high-fidelity platform dedicated to the development of new quantum algorithms for chemistry, enabling the modeling of realistic chemical systems at accuracies approaching the exact Full Configuration Interaction (FCI) / Complete Basis Set (CBS) limit.

</details>


### [109] [Programmable Signal Design for Quantum Phase Estimation via Quantum Signal Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01205)
*Zikang Jia,Suying Liu,Yulong Dong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于量子信号处理的量子相位估计可编程信号设计框架，通过优化信号形状提高灵敏度效率，保持海森堡极限缩放同时改善实际资源因子


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子相位估计是量子算法和传感的核心原语，现有方法主要依赖预定义的信号族，缺乏针对当前不确定性区域进行信号优化的能力，限制了信息获取效率

Method: 基于量子信号处理的可编程信号设计框架，将相位估计建模为最大-最小优化问题，引入灵敏度效率参数量化每查询深度的信息增益，结合优化的量子信号变换和结构化经典推理

Result: 数值结果显示相比鲁棒相位估计等标准协议，该框架降低了估计方差，保持海森堡极限缩放的同时提高了灵敏度效率和实际资源因子

Conclusion: 建立了通过可编程信号整形实现量子-经典协同设计的新范式，该框架可扩展到高维哈密顿量本征值估计，为量子相位估计提供了更高效的信号优化方法

Abstract: Quantum phase estimation is a central primitive in quantum algorithms and sensing, where performance is governed by the sensitivity of measurement signals to the target parameter. While existing methods have developed increasingly sophisticated inference and adaptive design strategies, the signal family used for phase learning is often largely pre-specified. Here we propose a programmable signal design framework for quantum phase estimation based on quantum signal processing, which enables the measurement signal to be tailored to the current uncertainty region. We cast phase estimation as a max-min optimization problem over admissible signals and introduce a sensitivity efficiency parameter that quantifies information gain per query depth. The resulting iterative algorithm combines optimized quantum signal transformations with structured classical inference, retaining Heisenberg-limited scaling while improving sensitivity efficiency and practical resource prefactors. Numerical results show reduced estimation variance compared with standard protocols such as robust phase estimation. Our framework also extends to Hamiltonian eigenvalue estimation in higher dimensions and establishes a quantum-classical co-design paradigm through programmable signal shaping.

</details>

### [110] [Crystals Caught Doping: Metallic Wigner Crystals in Rhombohedral Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00114)
*Junkai Dong,Tomohiro Soejima,Daniel E. Parker,Ashvin Vishwanath*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究了Wigner晶体在弱钉扎下从绝缘性向金属性的转变，提出了自掺杂机制导致非公度金属Wigner晶体的形成，并在多层石墨烯中验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Wigner晶体多为公度相，在弱钉扎下会变成绝缘体。研究者希望理解何时公度Wigner晶体会自发掺杂产生巡游载流子，形成非公度金属Wigner晶体，从而解释实验中观察到的金属性行为。

Method: 提出了公度晶体不稳定性的通用判据，涉及电荷能隙与"堆积偏置"的竞争。将该理论应用于菱面体多层石墨烯，通过自洽Hartree-Fock计算非公度晶体的相图。

Result: 计算显示公度晶体存在自掺杂不稳定性。相图显示金属Wigner晶体相直接邻接绝缘Wigner晶体相。该理论能自然解释菱面体石墨烯中Wigner晶体相附近观察到的霍尔电导反转现象。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Wigner晶体从绝缘相向金属相转变的理论框架，解释了多层石墨烯中的实验观测，为理解电子晶体的金属性行为提供了新视角。

Abstract: Nearly a century after Wigner's initial proposal, electron crystals are now a topic of intense experimental and theoretical interest. However, most proposed crystalline phases are commensurate and therefore become insulating in the presence of even weak pinning. In this work we discuss when a commensurate Wigner crystal will spontaneously self dope and develop itinerant carriers, giving rise to an incommensurate and thus metallic Wigner crystal (MWC). We develop a general criterion for the instability of the commensurate crystal which involves the competition between the charge gap at commensurability and a ``packing bias'' whose sign selects whether electron or hole doping is preferred. We then apply these insights to rhombohedral multilayer graphene, where calculations for commensurate crystals reveal instabilities towards self-doping. Carrying out self-consistent Hartree-Fock over the landscape of incommensurate crystals reveals the phase diagram, where a broad MWC phase appears directly adjacent to an insulating Wigner crystal phase. Recent observations of an island of reversed Hall conductance near a putative Wigner crystal phase in rhombohedral graphene are naturally explained by our theory.

</details>


### [111] [Magnetoelectric Control of Toroidal Moment in Ferroaxial Crystal PbMn$_{2}$Ni$_{6}$Te$_{3}$O$_{18}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00526)
*Shungo Aoyagi,Shunsuke Kitou,Taka-hisa Arima,Yusuke Tokunaga*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过电场和磁场控制PbMn₂Ni₆Te₃O₁₈中的磁环矩取向，利用定向二色性可视化磁铁环畴，揭示磁环矩、晶体铁轴矩和磁电单极子之间的内在耦合关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁性多极序破坏某些对称性并常激活独特的物理性质，如非互易定向二色性和线性磁电效应。研究旨在探索控制磁环矩取向的方法，并理解磁环矩、铁轴矩和磁电单极子之间的耦合机制。

Method: 在PbMn₂Ni₆Te₃O₁₈材料中，通过两种不同配置施加电场和磁场来控制磁环矩取向。利用定向二色性技术可视化磁铁环畴，从而研究磁环矩、晶体铁轴矩和磁电单极子之间的内在耦合关系。

Result: 成功实现了通过电场和磁场对磁环矩取向的控制，并利用定向二色性技术可视化展示了磁铁环畴。实验证实了磁环矩、晶体铁轴矩和磁电单极子之间存在内在耦合关系。

Conclusion: 该研究不仅提供了控制磁环矩的有效途径，还为研究铁轴序提供了一种新方法。揭示了铁性多极序中不同序参量之间的耦合机制，对理解和设计具有独特物理性质的多功能材料具有重要意义。

Abstract: Ferroic multipole orders break some symmetry and often activate unique physical properties, such as nonreciprocal directional dichroism and linear magnetoelectric effects. Here, we demonstrate the control of magnetic toroidal orientation in PbMn$_{2}$Ni$_{6}$Te$_{3}$O$_{18}$ through the application of electric and magnetic fields in two distinct configurations. Through directional dichroism, we successfully visualize magnetic ferrotoroidic domains, establishing the intrinsic coupling among magnetic toroidal moment, crystallographic ferroaxial moment, and magnetoelectric monopoles. Our findings not only present an effective pathway for controlling magnetic toroidal moment but also provide a novel approach for investigating ferroaxial ordering.

</details>


### [112] [The $\mathbb{Z}_N^{\times 3}$ symmetry protected boundary modes in two-dimensional Potts paramagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00910)
*Hrant Topchyan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 基于三角晶格上具有ℤ_N^×3对称性的二维对称保护拓扑相，构建并分析了一类一维边界哈密顿量，其结构由N的算术性质决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二维对称保护拓扑相在一维边界上的实现，探索边界理论如何反映体相的拓扑性质，特别是对称性在边界上的反常实现方式。

Method: 使用基于上同调的变换方法，从二维晶格模型显式导出边界模式，分析边界哈密顿量的代数结构，研究N为质数和合数时的不同行为。

Result: 对于质数N，边界理论可用相互对易的Temperley-Lieb代数描述；对于合数N，模型呈现分层或因子化结构；所有相可通过主模型加上局域缺陷自由度来理解，全局对称性在边界上以非局域和反常方式实现。

Conclusion: 二维对称保护拓扑相的边界理论结构由对称群的算术性质决定，边界对称性实现具有反常性，直接体现了对应的't Hooft反常，为理解拓扑相的边界行为提供了系统框架。

Abstract: We construct and analyze a class of one-dimensional boundary Hamiltonians arising from two-dimensional symmetry-protected topological phases with $\mathbb{Z}_N^{\times 3}$ symmetry on a triangular lattice. Using a cohomology-based transformation, the lattice models for the edge modes are explicitly obtained, and their structure is shown to be governed by the arithmetic properties of $N$. For prime $N$, the boundary theory admits a formulation in terms of mutually commuting Temperley-Lieb algebras. For the composite values of $N$, the models exhibit hierarchical or factorized structures. We demonstrate that all phases can be understood in terms of primary models augmented by local defect degrees of freedom that partition the system into independent segments. Finally, the global symmetry is realized on the boundary in a non-on-site and anomalous manner via a projective representation, directly realizing the corresponding 't Hooft anomaly.

</details>


### [113] [Excitations across the equilibrium and photoinduced `hidden' states of magnetoresistive manganites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00991)
*Shiyu Fan,Feng Jin,Taehun Kim,Umesh Kumar,Zixun Zhang,Vivek Bhartiya,Jiemin Li,Brandon Yalin,Yanhong Gu,Mingqiang Gu,Wen Hu,Claudio Mazzoli,G. Lawrence Carr,Osor S. Barišić,Andrey S. Mishchenko,Valentina Bisogni,Sobhit Singh,Wenbin Wu,Jonathan Pelliciari*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过超快激光激发与共振非弹性X射线散射技术，揭示了La₂/₃Ca₁/₃MnO₃中光诱导"隐藏"相的特性，发现其具有软化极化子激发、部分抑制Jahn-Teller畸变等独特特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超快激光脉冲产生的"隐藏"相在量子材料中的性质，特别是La₂/₃Ca₁/₃MnO₃中光诱导相变的本质和激发特性，这些相具有长寿命和可逆性，对技术应用具有吸引力。

Method: 结合近红外超快光激发与原位输运测量、X射线吸收谱和共振非弹性X射线散射，对比应变和温度调控的热力学相与光激发相，通过改变温度、外延应变和光激发通量构建极化子相图。

Result: 在热力学体系中建立了极化子与输运的关联，将其置于Holstein模型的"强耦合"区域；在光激发的LCMO-AFI中发现长寿命相，其特征为极化子激发软化、Jahn-Teller畸变部分抑制而声子几乎不变；构建了极化子相图并识别了各相的关键光谱特征。

Conclusion: 激光-RIXS方法为非频闪条件下探索量子材料中的光诱导"隐藏"相建立了通用平台，揭示了平衡相图中不存在的光激发态，为理解光诱导相变机制提供了新见解。

Abstract: "Hidden" phases, generated using ultrafast laser pulses (few hundred femtoseconds), with properties distinct from thermodynamic equilibrium, are appealing for technologies because they can be long-lived, with lifetimes of hours or weeks, and reversible with temperature sweeping or extra pulses. In this regard, La$_{2/3}$Ca$_{1/3}$MnO$_3$ (LCMO) stands out due to its tunability through epitaxial strain, which can drive the bulk ferromagnetic metal (FMM) into an antiferromagnetic insulator (AFI), and its susceptibility to photo-induced transitions. Indeed, AFI LCMO displays a long-lived photo-induced transition into a putative 'hidden' phase whose exact nature and excitations are still largely unknown. Here, we combine ultrafast photo-excitation in the near infrared with in situ transport, x-ray absorption (XAS), and Resonant Inelastic X-ray Scattering (RIXS) to investigate the excitations (polarons, phonons, and orbital) of the photo-excited phase of LCMO and contrast them with the thermodynamic phases achieved through strain and temperature. In the thermodynamic regime, we establish the correlation between polarons and transport, placing them in the 'strong coupling' regime of the Holstein model. Upon photo-excitation of LCMO-AFI, we uncover a long-lived phase characterized by the softening of the polaron excitations, the partial suppression of the Jahn-Teller distortion, and nearly unchanged phonons, showing the emergence of a photo-excited state absent in the equilibrium phase diagram. Finally, by varying temperature, epitaxial strain, and photo-excitation fluence, we construct a polaron phase diagram and identify the key spectroscopic signatures of each phase. Our laser-RIXS approach establishes a versatile platform for exploring photo-induced 'hidden' phases in quantum materials in non-stroboscopic conditions.

</details>


### [114] [The multichannel Dyson equation for double ionisation spectroscopies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01085)
*Pierre Sellié,J. Arjan Berger,Pina Romaniello*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种超越随机相位近似的方法，通过多通道Dyson方程耦合粒子-粒子二体格林函数与四体格林函数的3空穴-1电子和3电子-1空穴通道，从而能够同时描述光电子能谱中的准粒子和卫星峰特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法使用随机相位近似来模拟涉及双电离过程的光电子能谱（如俄歇光谱），但这种方法只能捕获准粒子特征，无法描述卫星峰。需要发展超越RPA的方法来完整描述光电子能谱。

Method: 采用多通道Dyson方程，将粒子-粒子二体格林函数与四体格林函数的3空穴-1电子和3电子-1空穴通道耦合，从而在RPA基础上纳入更多相关效应。

Result: 通过多通道Dyson方程方法，能够同时描述光电子能谱中的准粒子和卫星峰特征，超越了传统RPA方法只能描述准粒子的局限性。

Conclusion: 多通道Dyson方程为模拟涉及双电离过程的光电子能谱提供了一种有效方法，能够更完整地描述光谱特征，包括传统方法无法捕获的卫星峰结构。

Abstract: Several photoemission spectroscopies and, in particular, Auger spectroscopy, involve double-ionization processes. For the numerical simulation of these spectroscopies it is convenient to use the particle-particle channel of the two-body Green's functions since its poles correspond to excitation energies in which the final state has two more particles (holes or electrons) than the initial state. In standard approaches it is approximated within the random phase approximation. As a consequence only the quasiparticles of the photoemission spectrum are captured but none of the satellites features. In this work, we go beyond this approximation by employing the multichannel Dyson equation. By coupling the particle-particle two-body Green's function to the 3-hole-1-electron and 3-electron-1-hole channels of the four-body Green's function, the multichannel Dyson equation incorporates correlations beyond the RPA in a straightforward way. We are thus able to describe both quasiparticles and satellites in the photoemission spectra.

</details>


### [115] [Electronic structure and correlation of La$_4$Co$_2$NiO$_8$Cl$_2$: a theoretical proposal for a La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$-like high-temperature superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01223)
*Si-Yong Jia,Jing-Xuan Wang,Jian-Hong She,Rong-Qiang He,Zhong-Yi Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究基于高温超导镍酸盐的发现，提出并理论预测了一种钴基La₄Co₂NiO₈Cl₂材料可能具有高温超导性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于双层镍酸盐La₃Ni₂O₇的高温超导发现，以及随后在三层镍酸盐La₄Ni₃O₁₀高压下发现超导性，研究人员受到启发，探索钴基类似材料作为潜在的高温超导体。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论结合动力学平均场理论（DFT+DMFT）方法，对高压下三层钴酸盐La₄Co₃O₁₀进行电子掺杂，研究新化合物La₄Co₂NiO₈Cl₂的晶体结构和强关联电子结构。

Result: 研究发现，La₄Co₂NiO₈Cl₂的晶体结构和强关联电子结构与高压下的La₄Ni₃O₁₀相似，这表明该新化合物可能具有高温超导性。

Conclusion: 通过理论计算预测的钴基La₄Co₂NiO₈Cl₂材料在结构和电子性质上与已知的高温超导镍酸盐类似，为实验探索新的高温超导材料提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Based on the discovery of high-temperature superconductivity in the bilayer nickelate La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$, several Co-based La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$-like materials were theoretically predicted as possible high-temperature superconductors by electron doping. Motivated by these findings and the subsequent discovery of superconductivity in the trilayer nickelate La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$ under high pressure, we propose and investigate a Co-based La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$-like material. With electron doping to the high-pressure trilayer cobaltate La$_4$Co$_3$O$_{10}$, using density functional theory combined with dynamical mean-field theory (DFT+DMFT), we find that the resulting compound La$_4$Co$_2$NiO$_8$Cl$_2$ exhibits a crystal structure and a strongly correlated electronic structure similar to those of La$_4$Ni$_3$O$_{10}$ under high pressure. This suggests that this new compound may host high-temperature superconductivity.

</details>

### [116] [Dielectric response and viscosity due to dipolar interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00262)
*David S. Dean,Haim Diamant*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文建立了强极性液体中粘度与介电函数之间的预测性联系，揭示了介电弛豫与粘度的直接关系，并解释了双弛豫时间现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上液体的介电响应和粘度被视为独立性质分别处理。本文旨在揭示在强极性液体中这两种基本性质之间的内在联系，重新审视德拜提出的液体动力学经典思想。

Method: 采用随机场理论处理热偶极场动力学与流体力学流动的耦合，推导了可观测量对流动响应的广义Kubo关系。在此基础上，建立了基于体力相关函数而非传统应力张量的Green-Kubo粘度公式，并推导了偶极相互作用对粘度的贡献。

Result: 在强极性液体（如水）中，由热范德华相互作用产生的粘性耗散是主要耗散机制，导致介电弛豫与粘度之间的直接联系。理论还预测即使只有一个微观弛豫机制，介电响应中也会出现第二个弛豫时间。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了介电性质与粘度之间的预测性联系，为解释液体介电谱中普遍观察到的双弛豫时间现象提供了自然解释，并为识别电化学储能中具有前景的溶剂提供了实用途径。

Abstract: The dielectric response and viscosity are two fundamental properties of liquids that are usually treated separately. Here we show that in highly polar liquids the viscosity can be predicted directly from the dielectric function. We employ a stochastic field theory for thermal dipole-field dynamics coupled to hydrodynamic flow, and derive a very general Kubo relation for the response of an observable to the flow. We then use this to derive a Green-Kubo formula for the viscosity operator in terms of the correlation function for the body force, rather than the usual stress tensor formulation, and from this we derive the contribution to the viscosity due to dipolar interactions. In strongly polar liquids like water we show that viscous dissipation arising from these thermal van der Waals interactions is the dominant dissipative mechanism, leading to a direct connection between dielectric relaxation and viscosity. The theory also predicts the emergence of a second relaxation time in the dielectric response even when only a single microscopic relaxation mechanism is present. This additional timescale contributes to the intrinsic Debye relaxation and provides a natural explanation for the widespread empirical observation that many liquids require two relaxation times to fit their dielectric spectra. By establishing a predictive link between dielectric properties and viscosity, our results revisit classical ideas of liquid dynamics originating with Debye and suggest a practical route for identifying promising solvents for electrochemical energy storage.

</details>


### [117] [Self-similar summation of virial expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00294)
*V. I. Yukalov,E. P. Yukalova*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出基于自相似近似理论的新方法，用于对发散的状态方程维里展开进行求和，克服传统Padé求和的缺陷


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 维里展开在有限密度下通常发散，传统Padé求和方法存在非唯一性、虚假极点等问题，需要更可靠的求和方案

Method: 基于自相似近似理论，构建正则且唯一确定的求和方案，无需拟合参数，能够识别物理相关的极点

Result: 自相似近似精度不低于带拟合参数的最佳Padé近似或蒙特卡洛模拟，在某些情况下能精确重构目标函数

Conclusion: 自相似求和为维里展开提供了一种优于传统Padé方法的替代方案，具有正则性、唯一性和物理合理性

Abstract: Virial expansions are the series in powers of density assumed to be small. However, the equations of state require to consider finite densities for which virial expansions, as a rule, diverge. In order to extrapolate a virial expansion to the values, where this expansion diverges, one uses summation methods. The most often used method is the Padé summation, which has several deficiencies. First of all, Padé approximants are not uniquely defined, suggesting a large table of admissible variants. Second, often there appear spurious unphysical poles. On the contrary, in those cases where the existence of a pole is physically motivated, Padé approximants do not necessarily exhibit it. A new approach for the summation of virial expansions is suggested, based on self-similar approximation theory. The method is regular and uniquely defined. It allows for the determination of physically motivated poles. The accuracy of self-similar approximants is not worse than that of the best Padé approximants with fitting parameters or of Monte Carlo simulations. The self-similar summation is based solely on virial expansions, involving no fitting parameters. In some cases, self-similar summation allows for reconstructing the sought functions exactly. The approach is illustrated by summing virial expansions for hard-disk fluids, hard-sphere fluids, and systems with power-law potentials.

</details>


### [118] [Unified Gauge-Geometry Symmetry for Equilibrium Statistical Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00354)
*Hai Pham-Van*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出基于对称性的平衡统计力学框架，将传统时空对称性与相空间规范平移不变性统一为单一李群，推导出广义Ward恒等式和交叉关系，为多体系统提供一致的组织基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计力学中的对称性处理通常分离考虑时空对称性和内部对称性，缺乏统一的框架。作者旨在建立一个综合的对称性框架，将传统时空对称性（平移、旋转、伽利略提升、膨胀、粒子交换）与相空间规范平移不变性统一起来，为多体系统的结构、力学和动力学提供一致的对称性视角。

Method: 采用基于对称性的框架，利用Noether定理推导广义Ward恒等式和交叉关系。构建单一李群结构，将传统时空对称性与相空间规范平移不变性相结合。提出Wigner-Eckart-Ward约化方法简化张量-超力关联函数，并概述满足Ward约束的等变规范约束密度泛函理论（DFT）公式。

Result: 获得一组广义Ward恒等式和先前未探索的交叉关系，这些关系源于不同对称生成元的非对易性。建立了包含已知求和规则的精确恒等式层次结构，并指出了不同响应和关联函数之间可能的交叉耦合关系。在各项同性流体中，张量-超力关联函数可简化为两个标量径向谱。

Conclusion: 该框架为液体、混合物和界面现象提供了一致的组织基础，可能为多体系统的结构、力学和动力学提供基于对称性的统一视角，连接了平衡统计力学中的不同对称性概念。

Abstract: We present a symmetry-based framework for equilibrium statistical mechanics that formulates a single Lie group combining conventional spacetime symmetries with a recently identified phase-space gauge-shifting invariance [Muller et al., Phys. Rev. Lett. 133, 217101 (2024)]. Using Noether's theorem, we obtain a set of general Ward identities together with previously unexplored cross-relations arising from the noncommutation of different symmetry generators. The approach extends standard many-body symmetries, such as translations, rotations, Galilean boosts, dilations, and particle exchange, by incorporating an internal gauge-shift symmetry within a unified group structure. The resulting Lie algebra suggests a hierarchy of exact identities that encompass established sum rules and indicate possible cross-coupling relations between distinct response and correlation functions. We also identify a Wigner-Eckart-Ward reduction that simplifies tensor-hyperforce correlators to two scalar radial spectra in isotropic fluids, and we outline an equivariant gauge-constrained DFT formulation whose Euler-Lagrange equations are constructed to satisfy the corresponding Ward and cross-Ward constraints. This framework provides a consistent organizational basis for phenomena in liquids, mixtures, and interfaces, and may offer a symmetry-based perspective connecting structure, mechanics, and dynamics in many-body systems.

</details>


### [119] [Anomalous waiting-time distributions in postselection-free quantum many-body dynamics under continuous monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00358)
*Kazuki Yamamoto,Ryusuke Hamazaki*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究连续监测量子多体系统中量子跳跃的等待时间分布，发现半链子系统WTD存在异常尾部，与全系统的泊松分布形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在无条件动力学导致平凡无限温度态的连续监测量子多体系统中，子系统量子跳跃等待时间分布的特性，探索其与全系统行为的差异。

Method: 通过分析超算符$\mathscr L_0$的谱特性来研究半链子系统WTD，该算符通过从完整Liouvillian中移除与半链子系统相关的跳跃项定义。研究测量强度对系统尺寸依赖性的影响。

Result: 半链子系统WTD表现出异常尾部，偏离泊松分布。长时间行为由$\mathscr L_0$的最大实部特征值$λ_0$主导。发现测量强度导致系统尺寸依赖性的定性变化：弱测量时$λ_0$与系统尺寸成比例减小，强测量时$λ_0$与系统尺寸无关。

Conclusion: 建立了理解监测量子动力学中子系统非平凡WTD的谱框架，为评估多体效应对WTD的影响提供了新的诊断工具，且WTD可从量子跳跃时空记录中提取，无需后选择即可实验访问。

Abstract: We investigate waiting-time distributions (WTDs) of quantum jumps in continuously monitored quantum many-body systems, whose unconditional dynamics lead to the trivial infinite-temperature state. We demonstrate that the WTD of a half-chain subsystem exhibits an anomalous tail, markedly deviating from the Poissonian distribution in stark contrast to that of the whole system. By analyzing the spectral properties of the superoperator $\mathscr L_0$, which is defined by removing the jump terms associated with the half-chain subsystem from the full Liouvillian, we find that the long-time behavior with the anomalous tail of the half-chain WTD is governed by the eigenvalue $λ_0\:(<0)$ with the largest real part. We further reveal a qualitative change in the system-size dependence of $λ_0$ as a function of the measurement strength: for sufficiently weak measurement, $λ_0$ decreases proportionally to the system size, while for strong measurement, $λ_0$ scales independently of the system size, signaling the persistence of the anomalous half-chain WTD in the thermodynamic limit. The WTD is extracted solely from the spacetime record of quantum jumps $\{t_i,x_i\}$ and can be experimentally accessed without postselection. Our work establishes a spectral framework for understanding nontrivial WTDs in subsystems of monitored quantum dynamics and provides a novel diagnostics to assess many-body effects on WTDs.

</details>


### [120] [Emergent Macroscopic Nonreciprocity from Identical Active Particles via Spontaneous Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00552)
*Wei-Chen Guo,Zuo Wang,Pei-Fang Wu,Li-Jun Lang,Bao-Quan Ai,Liang He*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 单物种非互易性在有序相中通过自发对称破缺增强，产生宏观非互易性，促进行波带形成并驱动新型实空间凝聚


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究微观单物种非互易性在宏观尺度上的影响机制，探索自发对称破缺如何增强这种非互易性的宏观效应

Method: 使用具有视野锥和外噪声的单物种Vicsek模型，分析有序相中自发对称破缺对微观非互易性的增强作用

Result: 自发对称破缺显著增强微观单物种非互易性的宏观效应，产生宏观非互易性，促进行波带形成并驱动新型"旅行线"实空间凝聚

Conclusion: 自发对称破缺是微观单物种非互易性产生强宏观影响的基本机制，为复杂系统中非互易性研究提供新视角

Abstract: Nonreciprocity is known to generate a wide range of exotic phenomena in multi-species many-body systems, where different species influence one another through couplings that violate Newton's third law. In contrast, in the absence of explicitly imposed macroscopic nonreciprocal processes, single-species nonreciprocity -- another distinct form of nonreciprocity -- typically plays only a limited role in shaping macroscopic physics. Here, using a single-species Vicsek model with a vision cone and extrinsic noise, we show that spontaneous symmetry breaking (SSB) can dramatically enhance the macroscopic consequences of microscopic single-species nonreciprocity. In the ordered phase, this enhancement gives rise to an emergent macroscopic nonreciprocity that induces the system of identical active particles to admit an effective description with a "two-species" non-Hermitian structure. The resulting SSB-enhanced nonreciprocity substantially promotes traveling-band formation and, more strikingly, drives a novel real-space condensation of identical active particles, characterized by a "traveling line" with vanishing longitudinal width. Our findings uncover a fundamental mechanism by which microscopic single-species nonreciprocity can exert strong macroscopic influences in complex systems.

</details>


### [121] [Tunable information insulation induced by constraint mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00732)
*Akshay Panda,Anwesha Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究由两个具有对偶约束的1D PXP链组成的复合模型，其中连接点作为量子信息交换的无限运动学势垒。通过放松连接点约束可使势垒可渗透，多个冻结连接点将希尔伯特空间分裂为指数增长的Krylov碎片，每个对称性分辨扇区的能级统计严格为泊松分布。研究发现存在手性保护的零能模，以及非零能疤痕态数量的倍增，并引入了具有空间可调热和非热区域的新型Fock态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有对偶约束的复合PXP链系统中量子信息传输的调控机制，探索通过工程化缺陷控制希尔伯特空间分裂、量子态保护和热化行为的新方法，为可编程Rydberg原子平台实现复杂量子架构提供理论基础。

Method: 构建两个1D PXP链的复合模型，引入连接点作为量子信息交换的无限运动学势垒。通过放松连接点约束使势垒可渗透，研究多个冻结连接点导致的希尔伯特空间Krylov碎片化。分析对称性分辨扇区的能级统计，研究手性保护零能模的特性，探索非零能疤痕态的倍增机制，并设计具有空间可调热和非热区域的新型Fock态。

Result: 连接点作为完美反射体阻止量子信息泄漏，放松约束后变为可渗透。多个冻结连接点导致希尔伯特空间指数分裂为Krylov碎片。每个对称性分辨扇区的能级统计严格为泊松分布，表明哈密顿量的张量和是子PXP链混沌谱的纯叠加。存在手性保护的零能模，在物理边界和连接点附近的体区有局域峰。非零能疤痕态数量倍增。成功设计出具有空间可调热和非热区域的新型Fock态。

Conclusion: 该复合PXP链系统展示了通过工程化缺陷控制量子信息传输、希尔伯特空间分裂和热化行为的强大能力。手性保护零能模和倍增的疤痕态为量子态保护提供了新机制。提出的架构可直接在可编程Rydberg原子平台中实现，为量子模拟和量子信息处理提供了新的实验方案。

Abstract: We study a composite model of two $1D$ $PXP$ chains with dual constraints, forming a junction that acts as an infinite kinematic barrier to quantum information exchange. Moreover, the hard wall at the junction which acts as a perfect reflector, preventing any quantum information leakage between the two sides of the composite chain, can be made permeable by relaxing the constraint at the junction sites. Multiple frozen junctions shatter the Hilbert space into disjoint Krylov fragments, the number of which increases exponentially with the engineered defects. Furthermore, the energy level statistics in each symmetry-resolved sector are strictly Poissonian, demonstrating that the tensor sum of the disjoint Hamiltonians results in a pure superposition of the chaotic spectra of the sub- $PXP$ chains. We also find that a chirally protected zero-energy mode can exist which has local peaks at the physical edges and within the bulk near the junction sites. This state is protected from hybridization with bulk states induced by any chirality preserving disorder. Due to the tensor product structure of the eigenfunctions, the non-zero energy scar states also multiply in number. Finally, we introduce novel Fock states with spatially tunable thermal and athermal regions. This architecture can be readily realized in programmable Rydberg atom platforms using optical tweezers, addressing beams and facilitation techniques.

</details>


### [122] [Negative Differential Heat Conductivity in a Harmonic Chain Coupled to a Particle Reservoir](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00777)
*Simon Krekels,Christian Maes,Ion Santra,Ruoxun Zhai*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文证明负微分热导率可以纯粹源于热浴的性质及其与介质的耦合，无需非线性相互作用。通过构建由过阻尼热粒子组成的浴与谐波链耦合，观察到显著的负微分热导率效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上认为负微分热导率需要连接介质中的非线性相互作用。本文旨在证明这种效应可以纯粹源于热浴的性质及其与系统的耦合方式，即使在原本线性的系统中也能出现。

Method: 构建一个由过阻尼热粒子组成的浴，将其耦合到谐波链的一端，另一端连接标准的朗之万热浴。通过分析稳态热流来研究热输运特性，计算有效耗散系数。

Result: 观察到显著的负微分热导率：当两个热浴之间的温差发散时，通过链的稳态热流消失。在高温极限下，有效耗散系数与粒子浴温度的平方成反比，导致浴与链之间的渐近解耦。

Conclusion: 非平衡输运性质可以强烈地受环境结构及其与系统耦合方式的影响，即使在原本线性的系统中也是如此。负微分热导率不一定需要非线性相互作用，而可以纯粹源于热浴的特性。

Abstract: When coupling thermal baths at different temperatures, negative differential thermal conductivity is typically attributed to nonlinear interactions in the connecting medium. In this work, we demonstrate that such an effect can arise purely from the nature of the thermal baths and their coupling with the medium. Specifically, we construct a bath composed of overdamped thermal particles, which is coupled to one end of a harmonic chain, while the other end is connected to a standard Langevin heat bath. By analyzing the steady-state heat current, we observe significant negative differential thermal conductivity. In particular, as the temperature difference between the two baths diverges, the steady-state heat current through the chain vanishes. The effect is thermokinetic: we compute the effective dissipative coefficient and we find that it scales inversely with the square of the temperature of the particle bath in the high-temperature limit, resulting in an asymptotic decoupling between the bath and the chain. Our results highlight that nonequilibrium transport properties can be strongly influenced by the structure of the environment and its coupling to the system, even in otherwise linear systems.

</details>


### [123] [Statistical Physics of Coding for the Integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00858)
*Neri Merhav*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究基于zeta分布的压缩编码范式，建立统计力学解释，提出接近理想码长的简单编码方案，并分析Hagedorn型相变对系综等价性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自然数压缩编码的新范式，通过zeta分布建立编码理论与统计力学之间的联系，探索素数对数作为能级的玻色气体系统的类比，旨在开发高效的压缩编码方案并理解其统计力学特性。

Method: 1. 基于zeta分布的自然数编码范式；2. 建立Hagedorn系统和以素数对数为能级的玻色气体的统计力学解释；3. 提出接近理想码长的简单编码方案；4. 推导自然数向量块编码的微正则熵函数；5. 分析大偏差率函数并确定最佳编码参数。

Result: 1. 微正则熵函数具有渐近线性，表现出类似Hagedorn系统的行为；2. 推导出大偏差率函数并提供了最佳编码参数的公式；3. 由于Hagedorn型相变导致配分函数定义域退化，仅存在部分系综等价性。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了zeta分布编码与统计力学系统之间的深刻联系，提出的编码方案接近理论最优，但Hagedorn相变限制了系综的完全等价性，为自然数压缩编码提供了新的理论框架和实用方法。

Abstract: We study a paradigm of coding for compression of the natural numbers via the zeta distribution and develop a statistical-mechanical interpretation, both in terms of Hagedorn systems and a Bose gas with energy levels given by logarithms of prime numbers. We also propose a simple coding scheme for the zeta distribution that nearly achieves the ideal code length. For block coding of vectors of natural numbers, we derive the micro-canonical entropy function and demonstrate its asymptotic linearity implying that its behavior is analogous to that of a Hagedorn system. We also derive the large deviations rate function, and provide a formula for the best coding parameter in the large deviations sense. We show that due the Hagedorn-type phase transition there is only partial equivalence of ensembles, due to the degeneration of the domain of the partition function.

</details>


### [124] [Absence of $O (2)$ symmetry in the Vicsek model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00930)
*Yushin Takahashi,Kota Mitsui,Tsuyoshi Mizohata,Hideyuki Miyahara*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视Vicsek模型，发现其缺乏O(2)对称性，并证明当自适应选择全局相位时，原始论文中报告的相变消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vicsek模型的相变被广泛认为与二维旋转对称性O(2)的自发对称性破缺相关，但作者发现原始Vicsek模型实际上缺乏O(2)对称性，需要重新评估其相变性质。

Method: 重新分析原始Vicsek模型，通过数值模拟验证模型对称性，并采用自适应全局相位选择方法来检验相变的存在性。

Result: 数值结果表明，当自适应选择全局相位时，原始Vicsek模型中报告的相变消失，证明该相变并非真正的对称性破缺相变。

Conclusion: 原始Vicsek模型缺乏O(2)对称性，因此其相变不应被解释为O(2)对称性的自发破缺，需要重新考虑该模型的相变机制。

Abstract: The phase transition in the Vicsek model is widely believed to be associated with spontaneous symmetry breaking of the two-dimensional rotational symmetry $O (2)$. In this paper, we revisit the original Vicsek model introduced in [Phys. Rev. Lett. 75, 1226] and demonstrate that the model lacks $O (2)$ symmetry. As a consequence, we numerically demonstrate that the phase transition reported in the original paper vanishes when the global phase is adaptively chosen.

</details>


### [125] [Principal component analysis of wavefunction snapshots in non-equilibrium dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01140)
*Dharmesh Yadav,Devendra Singh Bhakuni,Bijay Kumar Agarwalla*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 通过主成分分析研究波函数快照的非平衡量子动力学，提出特定变换最大化主成分信息含量并建立与可观测量联系，以海森堡自旋链为例验证并扩展至高阶关联提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非平衡量子动力学，探索如何通过无监督机器学习方法（特别是降维方案）从波函数快照数据集中提取物理信息，并建立与实验可观测量的联系。

Method: 对波函数快照数据集进行主成分分析，提出特定数据变换以最大化最大主成分的信息含量，建立主成分与物理可观测量之间的连接，以海森堡自旋链的不同初始状态量子动力学为例进行验证，并扩展方法以提取高阶关联。

Result: 成功建立了主成分与物理可观测量之间的连接，能够解释降维方案揭示的动力学特征，在海森堡自旋链模型中验证了方法的有效性，并展示了提取高阶关联的能力。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅适用于基于降维方案的其他无监督机器学习方法，而且与量子模拟器实验（包括高维系统）高度相关，为从实验数据中提取物理信息提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We study non-equilibrium quantum dynamics by performing principal component analysis on the data sets of wavefunction snapshots. We show that a specific transformation of the data sets maximizes the information content in the largest principal component and further enables its connection to certain observables. This connection enables us to explain the dynamical features revealed by such a dimensionality-reduction scheme. We demonstrate this using quantum dynamics of the Heisenberg spin chain, starting from different initial states, and further extend the approach to extract higher-order correlations. Our framework should also be applicable to other unsupervised machine-learning methods based on dimensionality-reduction schemes and is highly relevant to experiments with quantum simulators, including those in higher dimensions.

</details>
